$(function(){
    var $block = $('#j-bbs-index-last-block');
    page = 1;

    var $carousel = $block.find('#j-bbs-index-last-carousel');
    if ($carousel.length) {
        $carousel.owlCarousel({
            itemsCustom: [
                [320, 1],
                [600, 2],
                [767, 3],
                [991, 4]
            ]
        });
        // Custom Navigation Events
        $block.find('.j-next').click(function(){
            $carousel.trigger('owl.next');
        });
        $block.find('.j-prev').click(function(){
            $carousel.trigger('owl.prev');
        });
    }
    $('.sr-page__more').on('click', function(){
        data = {'page':page};
        bff.ajax('/item/indexlastblock',data, function(data){
            if(data.success){
                $('#del-j-bbs-index-last-carousel').append(data.html);
                page++;
                if(data.last || data.html==''){
                    $('.sr-page__more').hide();
                }
            }
        })
    });
});
